REGRESSION FIXTURES

What the defenses do

The lab checks these five known cases; it does not estimate real-world attack rates.

Five controlled attacks. Every outcome stays visible, including failure.

FixtureNaive BEFORE → AFTERTrustNet BEFORE → AFTER Defense goalCampaign false positivesNewcomer HOLD rate
brigade-denounceACCEPT → HOLD ACCEPT → ACCEPT Goal met 0.0% of 15 honest PRs 0.0%
sybil-cliquePROVISIONAL → ACCEPT PROVISIONAL → PROVISIONAL Goal met 0.0% of 15 honest PRs 0.0%

trusted-source-compromisePROVISIONAL → ACCEPT PROVISIONAL → ACCEPT Goal missed 0.0% of 15 honest PRs 0.0%
The boundary: a compromised trusted source.

A false endorsement from a directly trusted source can still produce ACCEPT. These synthetic regression fixtures establish specific behavior, not general attack resistance.

Decision rules and project limits

Strongest trust path multiplies edge weights and applies 0.6 decay per extra hop, up to 3 hops; alternate paths are not summed. Belief and disbelief combine trusted vouches and denunciations separately. They are support scores, not calibrated probabilities.

No live GitHub integration or Vouch-file adapter; organizer input compatibility is unverified. Advisory files are not signed. A compromised trusted source can defeat the defense.
